> Previous (2026-08-10, fourth session close) — **the citations question was put to Codex twice and the answer reframed the work.** WP4 is **DEFERRED by measurement**: the multi-alignment rate is 24.0% (not 7.0% — `n_spans` counts page hulls), and the "spurious piece" is usually either the work's own doublet (`תנ"ך, שמות` command//execution) or a compilation quoting its source — 34 works at ≥2x their length peers hold 53% of all conflicts. "Later work is probably the citation" was built twice and fires in the RIGHT direction, but the bake starved it 26,608→1,484 (both sides must already be `shipped`, and the verdict is unrecordable in the single `routing_reason` column). The deeper defect is **decision-to-surface integrity** (Codex's phrase): the findings chip is a constant ("Direct match" on 28,462 of 28,464 public main-pool rows), the expansion pane never reads the router, the one honesty warning dies on the v3 swap (108,235 rows → 0), and the public projection ships the quoter (`ילקוט שמעוני` 625 ids) while deleting the quoted work (`תנחומא` 0) and promoting 280 tie-broken rows. The owner's divergence statistic marks the off works and his own blind grades validate it (0 of 57 `ילקוט` rows correct; `ראב"ש` at #2/#3/#6/#14) — a curated quoter/quoted-inside flag seeded from that ranking is the proposed fix (curation sheet delivered). Session-4 record is inline below; no separate handout this session.

### Codebase-wide sys_id parser divergence (2026-08-25, found and fixed same day)

| Issue | File | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| **A `sys_id` was parsed out of a `raw_header` by ~24 hand-rolled regexes in two incompatible dialects** | `shared/`, `web/`, `scripts/` (24 sites) | ✅ Fixed (2026-08-25) | Reported from the multi-witness parallels review on `claude/antiochus-scroll-search-eval-uiesx5`, deliberately left there because it is corpus-wide. The report framed `97` as a possible NLI prefix and the divergence as latent. Both turned out to be wrong in ways that changed the fix. **`97` is the LOCAL "My Library" namespace** (Phase 95, `shared/local_sys_id.py`: `97` + machine_id(8) + content_hash(8)), generated on the desktop for a user's own files and deliberately disjoint from the corpus — not NLI. Measured on `libraries.csv`: **255,723 of 255,723 records begin `99`, all exactly 18 digits, including all 473 NLI-library rows.** So the corpus namespace is 99-only and the narrowing direction was right; but a single canonical form would have been wrong, because the repo genuinely has two namespaces. **The divergence was also not latent.** `re.search` scans anywhere, so the 99-only pattern applied to a LOCAL header does not miss cleanly — it can match a `99` sitting inside the LOCAL id's random digits and return a truncated, wrong sys_id: **6.36% of LOCAL ids, measured over 200,000**. `shared/lab_engine.py:1504` filters a result list that carries LOCAL LAB hits (CR-02/REQ-6). **CORRECTION (2026-08-26), superseding the original claim in this entry, in the commit message and in the PR body: that path was NOT a live defect.** The original text said the failure mode was excluding the wrong manuscript. It could not be. `excluded_set` holds resolved CORPUS sys_ids (`shared/exclusion_service.py::compute_excluded_ids`), which are always 18 digits, while a mis-match inside an 18-char LOCAL id is at most 16 digits (the `99` must start at index >= 2). So `m.group(1) in excluded_set` could never be true for a LOCAL row -- old and new both leave it unexcluded. And on corpus headers the old and new patterns are identical: 0 differing results over 50,000 generated headers. The mis-match is real but was inert at every migrated site traced (web sites cannot see LOCAL at all; `search_serializer` would at worst take an FJMS lookup miss on a bogus id before `_is_local_item` drops the row; `extract_unique_id` ingests corpus transcriptions only). The change is therefore HARDENING that removes a trap -- the truncation starts to matter the moment anything compares against ids that are not 18 digits -- not the live-bug fix originally described. The owner also ruled the desktop LOCAL-index verification out of scope as an unimportant edge case (2026-08-26), which this correction supports. FIX: `shared/sys_id_patterns.py` holds `CORPUS_SYS_ID_RE` (99-only) and `ANY_SYS_ID_RE` (99 or 97), both anchored with a leading `(?<!\d)` so a LOCAL header can never mis-fire; 22 sites moved to the corpus constant (incl. narrowing `shared/passage_parallels.py` and `web/services.py`, both web-only where LOCAL cannot exist), the two desktop parsers in `shared/metadata_manager.py` keep the agnostic one by design, and `web/start_content.py` is an annotated exemption (anchored full-string validator, not an extractor). GUARD: `tests/test_sys_id_patterns.py` repo-greps every tracked `.py` and fails on an unannotated hand-rolled pattern, pins each site to the shared object by identity, and pins the never-match-LOCAL property over 20,000 generated ids — proven able to fail by three separate mutations (re-widen a site; un-anchor the pattern; over-narrow a desktop parser), each failing distinctly. `scripts/check_sys_id_prefixes.py` re-takes the corpus measurement. Verified: 578 tests pass across the dependent test modules on the merged tree, with the same 10 failures + 11 errors present on clean `origin/master-main` (the masking sweep fails safe when `MASKING_SCAN_PATTERNS_FILE` is unset) -- confirmed in a throwaway worktree, so zero regressions. POST-REVIEW (same day): Codex found TWO P2 defects in `scripts/check_sys_id_prefixes.py`, both on the Tantivy branch -- the only path nothing ever executed, since it is skipped unless `--index` is passed AND the directory exists. (1) The repo root was never added to `sys.path`, so the documented invocation died on `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'shared'`. (2) The walk called `Searcher.segment_readers()`, which the Tantivy Python binding does not expose (it has `num_docs`/`num_segments`/`search`/`doc`). Both reproduced against a real 3-doc index, then fixed: the walk now pages `Query.all_query()` through `search(limit, offset)` and REFUSES to report clean off a short walk (`__incomplete__`), because a partial scan is indistinguishable from a corpus with no 97. Five tests added that build a real index and run the script as documented, incl. a control proving a seeded 97 fails the run; both original defects re-introduced as mutations and each caught. SECOND REVIEW ROUND (same day): Codex found TWO MORE P2 defects, both in the completeness guard added by the first round -- and both made the script answer "no new prefix here" without having looked. (a) `scanned` was incremented BEFORE the document was read, so an index whose schema lacks `full_header` balanced its own books and printed RESULT: OK off zero inspected headers. (Codex predicted an exception; the binding actually returns None for a missing field, so it lands on the no-sys_id path -- the fix catches both.) (b) DEEPER: prefix detection used `ANY_SYS_ID_RE`, i.e. the very constants the script exists to check, so a prefix outside 99/97 could not be matched at all -- an index of nothing but `98`-prefixed records reported clean. The script was blind to its own subject. Both reproduced, then fixed: detection now uses a prefix-AGNOSTIC pattern deliberately independent of the shared constants; every document lands in exactly ONE of classified/unreadable/unparsed and the three must sum to `searcher.num_docs`; the shared corpus pattern is additionally cross-checked against every real header it should match; and any hole in the walk makes a clean result impossible. Four more tests, incl. controls that a wrong-schema index and a `98` index each FAIL; both defects re-introduced as mutations and each caught by its own test. ALSO post-merge: `master-main` had meanwhile landed the multi-witness work, which introduced exactly the site the original report named -- `web/pages/parallels.py::WITNESS_SYS_ID_RE` and its `witness_sys_id` docstring, absent from both branches when this began. The repo lint caught it on the merge. Narrowed to the corpus constant and the docstring rewritten: its consolidation of three page-local copies into one was right, but the wide dialect rested on the same false premise, and once the engine it mirrors became corpus-only, staying wide would have recreated the divergence in the opposite direction. The page's `test_a_97_prefixed_manuscript_can_be_promoted` (whose fixture id `9700000001` was 10 digits -- not a valid LOCAL id either) was rewritten to keep its real invariant and joined by a test that a LOCAL header resolves to None rather than a truncated `993169503583183`. NOTE: the report's `web/pages/parallels.py::witness_sys_id` docstring and its `WITNESS_SYS_ID_RE:62` do not exist on either branch — that file carries 9 narrow sites and no wide constant. |

### Local full non-GUI suite segfault (2026-08-21, fixed same day)

| Issue | File | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Local full non-GUI suite dies nondeterministically past ~5,500 tests** | local pytest runs (`-m "not gui"`) | ❌ Open (found 2026-08-21) | ✅ Fixed (2026-08-21) | ROOT CAUSE: 34 desktop test files imported PyQt6 without the gui marker, so Qt widgets ran inside the "not gui" bucket and segfaulted the process once enough NiceGUI/asyncio tests shared it -- position-shaped (~68-72%) because that is the my_library/Qt alphabetical region. Bisected via alphabetically-interleaved thirds (two crashed at ~72% of themselves, one passed). FIX: all 34 files marked gui (pytestmark lists preserve existing skipifs), zero unmarked Qt imports remain by scripted self-check; both crashing segments then ran to completion (2,921 + 3,385 passed). The 4 residual failures are upstream v8.6.0 locale-dependent desktop tests (assert English labels on a Hebrew-locale machine) -- spun off as a separate task. Commit 484c8832. |

## v9.0.0 Discovery Milestone — status & coordination (2026-07-24; updated 2026-07-30)

- ✅ **Fixed (2026-08-19) — locus labels no longer repeat their own work title, and a rendered range no longer repeats an address level.** Closes BOTH doubling entries at once (the `daf_rif` prefix reported 2026-08-12 and the Tur/סמ"ג report of 2026-08-19), because they had one cause: `scripts/discovery_v4_build_reference.py::_locus_label` composes every chapter/section address as `f"{locus_title} {numeral}"` / `f"{locus_title}, {unit_label}"`, and `build_work_divisions.py` prefixes `רי"ף` to the `daf_rif` grain — while every surface that renders a locus renders the work title beside it. **Measured: 107 of the 679 locus works and 4,708 identifications, of which 44 works have been LIVE since the REF4 append** — never V4.2-specific; 2026-08-19 is just when the shape first landed on a work prominent enough for the owner to notice. FIXED AT THE SIDECAR INGESTION, not at the producer: the fully-qualified label is the right thing to STORE (the divisions DB is a hash-pinned build input whose labels are how a reviewer traces a unit back to the publisher's own reference), and rewriting it would mean rebuilding a pinned chain (V6 ← V5 ← V4 ← …) to change a display string. So `shared/discovery_locus.py::strip_work_title_prefix` takes the title off on the way into the asset — which also fixes `locus_unit.label_he` for the findings page's address filter, since `get_locus_units_enveloped` publishes that column directly, and not only the composed `locus_label`. The prefix is computed ONCE PER WORK from what every unit shares, never per label: a per-label draft corrupted the Zohar, where `ג` is a word of `ספר הזוהר, חלק ג` and the one daf out of 596 that happened to be ג rendered `ע"א`. Three further guards, each measured on the real 679 works: a lone division word is not a title (`ספר ג, עמ' 121` is "book 3" — the one wrong strip of the 108 the first rule produced); never empty; and distinctness is preserved per work, or the whole work reverts. `shorten_range_tail` now treats `/` as a word boundary too, so `ויקרא/כ–ויקרא/כג` reads `ויקרא/כ–כג`, and it slices the original tokens instead of rejoining with a space — which also fixes a latent bug that would have flattened `א/ד/ה` to `א ד ה`. **The build gate the 2026-08-12 entry asked for exists and was proven able to fail:** 9 mutations, each reverting one guard, each caught by a named test (`_tmp/prove_locus_gates.py`); the ingestion assertions read the ASSET's own `locus_unit.label_he`, not the helper's return value. The first harness was written in PowerShell and reported all five mutations green because the sources are CRLF and its string literals were LF, so every `.Replace()` silently matched nothing — the rewritten harness asserts the file actually changed before it runs a test.

  Superseded tracker text, kept verbatim (the `daf_rif` bullet's preamble
  half is NOT closed and stays in the tracker as its own entry):

  - ❌ **Open (2026-08-12, owner-reported from the A0c grading pass) — every `daf_rif` locus label embeds `רי"ף`, so a composed citation doubles it ("רי"ף יבמות, רי"ף יב ע"א").** Measured on the locus artifact: **all 1,717 units of all 25 `daf_rif` works** carry labels of the form `רי"ף א ע"א`, while the plain daf grains (`daf_bavli`, `daf2`) carry bare labels (`ב ע"א`) — the prefix is a grain-specific inconsistency, presumably added to mark Rif-pagination-vs-Bavli-daf, but the work title already says רי"ף on every surface that will ever render the label. Fix belongs to the locus BUILD (D-track, before the CD-batch bake, while no production consumer exists): default is the bare `daf_bavli` form (`יב ע"א`), with the Rif-pagination distinction carried by the title as it already is; a suffix form (`יב ע"א בדפי הרי"ף`) is the alternative if a label must stand alone. Whichever form, add a build gate asserting no unit label begins with a token of its own work's title, proven able to fail on today's data.
  - ❌ **Open (2026-08-19) — the restored Tur and סמ"ג locus labels EMBED their own work title, so a composed citation doubles it.** Same class as the `daf_rif` entry above, found by a post-rebuild spot-check through the real findings query: `ארבעה טורים, יורה דעה` renders `locus_label = 'ארבעה טורים, יורה דעה רפו'` and `ספר מצוות גדול (סמ"ג)` renders `'ספר מצוות גדול (סמ"ג), סמ"ג …'`, so a surface printing title + locus says the title twice. The three Zohar parts are CLEAN (`כא ע"א`, `צג ע"א`) — this is per-work, not systemic. Belongs with the `daf_rif` fix in the locus BUILD, under the same gate: assert no unit label begins with a token of its own work's title, proven able to fail on today's data. Cosmetic; deliberately not held for the deploy. Affects 4 of the 14 works restored by the `source_label` fix.

- ✅ **Done (2026-08-10) — the four owner rulings from the scholar audit are applied, and the ambiguity gate has been rebuilt because it could not have found any of them.** Commits `09565007` (division), `f8005b4f` (הלכה), `c8a582b3` + `129eb33c` (overlap), `b23a1b93` (counting word + headword), `a6370829` (the two source defects), `12156005` (the cross-item blind spot). Artifact: 824 works / 55,457 units, invariants clean, masking exit 0 over the repo AND cell-by-cell over the built SQLite.
  - **`ה` named five places, and fixing the label alone would have been a regression.** Mishneh Torah is Book → Hilkhot X → chapter and the chapter grain emitted the chapter alone: 1,657 units in 27 works carried a label another unit of the same work also carried. But `citation_pos` was the chapter's own VALUE, and `citation_runs` folds a span's pieces to the SET of positions and prints ONE label per position — so **11,559 of 29,148 real stored spans would have printed the WRONG הלכות or the wrong book** with the label fix and no repack (`ה` in הלכות שחיטה rendering as הלכות איסורי ביאה). Measured on the artifact, before and after. The division is carried exactly when the source states more than one, so the Bible family and every monolith child are untouched.
  - **The run-collapse was swallowing whole divisions.** It compared rendered labels, so where a label omitted the division it folded the end of one division into the start of the next: 8 boundaries in 5 works, including **the entire book of יונה living inside עובדיה's unit `א`**. It now compares the stated address; the build gains those 8 units back.
  - **THE GATE WAS STRUCTURALLY BLIND AND HAS BEEN REPLACED.** It grouped labels by citation position and flagged a label sitting at more than one — but the position collided in lockstep with the label, so it was comparing two names for one collapsed value. It read correctly, had been reviewed, and could not fire. `Unit` now carries `source_address`, the address the SOURCE states, as a tuple of levels, and three gates compare the render against it: a label naming two stated places, a citation position covering two, and — **in citation space, not table order, because that is where `citation_runs` merges** — a position that is a successor ACROSS a division boundary (22 real spans already rendered `ד–ה` running from הלכות קריית שמע into הלכות תפילה וברכת כוהנים). All three are proven able to fail **on the real corpus**, not only on fixtures.
  - **A blind spot that belonged to neither change that made it.** Renaming `משנה`→`הלכה` made the label map non-injective; carrying the stated address as division/chapter/sub dropped the KIND. Either alone is fine; together, two headers stating different sub-units at one number render one string AND record one address — and the gate compares exactly those two. Found only by a whole-queue pass. Latent today (0 of 13,059 four-column headers state `הלכה`) and closed by one extra tuple element.
  - **Grazing a unit is not witnessing it.** `units_for_span` now refuses a first unit the span reaches and barely touches: under 30 letters AND less than half the unit. Absolute rather than fractional is measured, not preferred — a 5%-of-unit rule discards first units holding up to 1,115 letters and drops more than half the span on 902 rows; the absolute rule on none. The half-unit guard was added after an adversarial pass found 11 real spans dropping a unit they witnessed at least half of and 3 they witnessed **entirely** (a 7-letter chapter removed for being small), and 407 of 55,449 units structurally unable to open a citation. As shipped: 11,893 fire, 11,892 citations move (5.08%), 0 emptied.
  - **A number that names nothing is not an address.** Eight staged works now say what their number counts (`מא` → `סימן מא`, `תריז` → `רמז תריז`) — 12,436 evidence rows. The Arukh could not be repaired that way (its staged number is a segment index no printed edition prints), so it is cited by HEADWORD: `שע–שעט` → `ערך שפץ–ערך שפשף`. That is **the one label in the builder derived from body text**, an exception written down as one; the filed letter is read off the file (modal first letter) rather than transliterated, 8,318 of 8,373 entries (99.3%) qualify, and the rest fall back to the ordinal. Five works whose counting unit could not be established keep the bare ordinal and are named in the code — waiting on a ruling, not overlooked.
- ✅ **Done (2026-08-09, commit `354e5ead`) — the discovery-v3 novelty axis is BOUGHT and BAKED, with the per-pair fingerprint gate armed.** The production run completed at the v3 grain: **$42.28** real spend, 5,659 calls, **67,079 of 67,079** shipped `(manuscript, work)` pairs judged, 2 retries. The re-assembled asset (`_tmp/v3_out2/discovery-v3.db`, `content_hash=d62ee5a2`) records `novelty_input_fingerprint_checked=1` with **67,079 fingerprint-OK, 0 mismatched, 0 unfingerprinted, 0 failed-closed** — the first v3 build that does not need `--novelty-allow-unfingerprinted-cache`.
  - **The price probe earned its keep.** A 30-case smoke measured `$0.0021`/case and projected **~$119** against a ~$41 authorization. A $5 hard-ceiling probe then priced 6,710 real cases at **$0.000745** (1.03× the historical rate), projecting $42.16 against an actual $42.28 — 0.3% out. The smoke was unrepresentative in the way that matters: cost per case **declines** across the run ($0.00093 → $0.00061 by quarter), so any short prefix over-projects. Do not price this gate from a smoke.
  - **New producer for a gate that had none:** `scripts/emit_novelty_input_fingerprints.py`. The builder's `--novelty-input-fingerprints` was documented and unimplementable — nothing emitted the file — so the named waiver was the only way to supply a cache. It recomputes fingerprints from the BUILD's own inputs, never from the verdict file's own stamped values (which would compare a file against itself and pass by construction). Emits **flat** `{key: fingerprint}`, the one shape `_load_novelty_fingerprints` accepts; provenance rides in a companion `.meta.json`.
  - **Mutation-proved, per the standing rule:** emitting witness-blind flips **8,125 of 67,079** pairs to MISMATCH; removing `known_witness_confidence` from `CACHE_KEY_FIELDS` collapses blind/high/low to one fingerprint and turns the new tests red.
  - **A real hole closed.** Every pre-existing per-field test in the gate-13 suite derives its expectation from what `render_batch` **sends**, and `known_witness_confidence` is never rendered — a high-confidence recorded witness is resolved by the heuristic funnel and the model is never called. It changes the ANSWER without changing the PROMPT, so the structural test skipped it (its assertion is guarded on `prompt_changed`). Deleting the field would have passed the whole suite while making $42.28 of verdicts reusable against a question they did not answer.
  - **Coverage is complete, not merely large:** shipped pairs = 67,079 = exactly the population judged. The remaining **64,693** `not_checked` rows are all `review_only` — never presented as discoveries, so correctly unjudged rather than silently skipped. `frame_content_hash` is byte-identical across the two assemblies (`dc15864d`), proving novelty was the only axis that moved.
  - **Not deployed.** The artifact is a local build; `discovery_data/manifest.json` is untouched and `DISCOVERY_ENABLED` is still unset in production.
  - **The mechanism exists and is not Bible-only.** `same_work_spike/probe/scripts/mask_ref_canon.py` masks, for every NON-canonical work (halakhic works included), the spans that align to the canonical index `CANON_CATS = {Bible, Mishnah, Tosefta, Bavli, Yerushalmi}`; `track1_match.py::build_ref_index` drops the masked seed grams.
  - **The gap is staleness, not design.** `ref_canon_masks_v2.json` was written **2026-07-10**. `ref2_build.py` expanded the reference corpus on **2026-07-15** to 326 REF2 works (247 of them undocumented `REF2:sef_*`), and `ref_corpus_v2.pkl` (5,849 works) is what G-launch3 loaded — the log confirms `ref 'glaunch3': 5849 works … mask 'ref_canon_masks_v2.json'`. **The masker was never re-run.** `REF2:sef_tur_orach_chaim` therefore has ZERO mask intervals, and the Isaiah text sits unmasked in its stream at raw offset 210191 — exactly where the 103-letter rows land.
  - **Two further holes even where a mask exists.** (1) Masking removes only SEED grams — "gram positions stay original, so span coordinates are unaffected" — so a hull seeded in unmasked flanking text extends straight through a masked verse during verification. Observed on the M-source edition `M:Ytext273001`, which IS masked (21 intervals, one covering the verse) yet still yields `rg0=251014` spans starting 78 letters before the interval. (2) **Liturgy is categorically outside `CANON_CATS`**, so a "liturgical formula + Bible verse" match defeats the mechanism twice over — which is precisely the owner's case.
  - **Fix requires re-running the masker AND re-matching**, so it is an owner call, not a code change. Until then, treat short matches on works that quote scripture as unscreened.
  - **Session-4 precise measurement:** the never-masked set is **268 works (247 `REF2:sef_*` + 21 `REF2:ja2_*`) = 38,050,533 letters = 33.3% of all non-canonical letters**. Worse, the canonical INDEX itself changed 92→252 works between mask and corpus, which `mapv2_mask_update.py`'s own header rules fatal — every carried-over mask is suspect; a clean rebuild is ~16h + ~3.5h re-match, and the re-mask also breaks the pinned frame fingerprint (`EXPECTED_FRAME_FP`).
  - **A post-hoc alternative exists and is strictly better on hole (1):** the gen-2 store carries `ref_work`/`ref_start`/`ref_end` non-null on ALL 502,498 evidence rows, so fresh masks can be intersected against STORED spans with no re-match — reaching hulls seeded in unmasked flanking text, which seed-dropping structurally cannot. Blast radius today: **20,721 shipped claims cite a never-masked work** (`ילקוט שמעוני` 8,454 + 4,840, `הלכות גדולות` 6,299, `תנא דבי אליהו רבה` 4,567, the owner's R. Tanhum case 2,902). What it cannot do: recover recall the index never produced.
  - **The owner's exact fear is measured unprotected (2026-08-11): ALL 23 `רבנו חננאל` works and ALL 36 `תוספות` works carry ZERO mask intervals** — both families sit in the never-masked 268, so nothing today stops a תוספות fragment being identified as רבנו חננאל (or vice versa) off the shared sugya alone. Design ruling from the same exchange: a match whose text is ONLY the shared canonical quote is a witness to the CANONICAL work (identify it as בבלי with a locus), never to either commentary — enforce via (1) the re-mask, (2) a post-hoc unique-remainder rule over the stored `ref_start`/`ref_end` (quote-share per evidence row; ~0 unique letters = not commentary evidence; same shape as gen-2 chrono's unique floor, no re-match needed), (3) the surface saying "shared text" on what survives. The rarity score of the entry above is the tiebreaker where the remainder is thin — רבנו חננאל is mostly paraphrase by construction, so thin remainders there are correct behaviour, not a bug.
  - **The three rules scored against the owner's 1,402 blind grades (2026-08-11; TRUE=correct/cowitness/partial 681, FALSE=quote_* /formula/wrong 717; canonical-claim losses priced 0 per owner).** QUOTER (work `diverges_work` share ≥50%, ≥15 checked): catches **46.2% of applicable FALSE at 83.5% kill-precision**, including **81 of the `quote_ba` class**; REGION (≥80% of matched letters in auto-derived ≥3x-enriched units): 25.5% at 77.1%; both together 47.5% at 83.8% (live router, in-sample on this very set: 80.1% at 84.9%). **Incremental value over the router: of the 143 FALSE rows it misses, the rules catch 51 (36%) — 22 of them `quote_ba`, the directional class nothing live touches.** The cost lands exactly where the owner predicted: 43 TRUE non-canonical rows killed, 27 of them witnesses of משנה תורה books (אהבה 13 / זמנים 10 / נשים 4) — the work-level flag cannot tell a siddur fragment matching the prayers from a real משנה-תורה fragment. **The refined composite with AUTO regions is WORSE, not better** (TP 207→129 for FP 41→36): machine-derived regions at the 0.8 threshold lose more catches than they spare — the owner's region NAMING pass is the unlock, not more geometry. **QUOTE (unique-remainder over the v2 masks) scored 0/351 BY CONSTRUCTION and is untestable on this set:** the graded rows survived a run that used these very masks at index time, so survivors are seeded outside them (max inside-share 0.27) — its target population is the 268 never-masked works, testable only after the re-mask. Probes: `probe_rules_vs_grades.py`, `probe_rules_followup.py` (session tmp).
  - **The ship plan exists and converged through five Codex adversarial rounds (2026-08-11): `_tmp/PLAN-granularity-ship.md` v7 FINAL, verdict APPROVE-WITH-CHANGES, conditions integrated.** Four contracts: (1) ONE rendered-relation precedence matrix on every surface and export path, recomputed per asset after public pruning, fail-closed on missing inputs, with a surface-grain mapping table for the four grains the repo renders relations at today; (2) citation coverage as NUMERIC per-family floors of verified citations over a ledgered denominator, with the pre-withholding population LOCKED and retention floors (overall ≥90%, per family ≥80%) whose breach BLOCKS the release; (3) a pass/fail rendered-output scholar audit — wrong-place, wrong-relation (direction error budget zero), and the TOO-BROAD granularity gate (≤15% per family) — with an overlap-aware frame that includes the 280 projection-promoted rows; (4) an executable audit-failure control plane (composable withholding predicates over materialized stratum membership, verifier-checked against the ledger). Evaluation: the 1,402 blind grades are DEV (used in rule design); the 400-row review pass is the FROZEN held-out; the bar is false rendered "direct witness" −50% with recall within 2 points.   - **A0-preflight RAN (2026-08-11) and the goal is reachable: 95.0% of eligible public rows already carry a scholar-citable place, 77.6% of them citing three units or fewer, 1.7% spanning a whole work.** Read-only against the representative PUBLIC asset (55,377 identifications / 613 works) + the locus artifact (1,390 works / 75,894 units); no schema landed. Report: `_tmp/PREFLIGHT-granularity.md`; the plan is amended to v8. **Measured floors**, unaddressable rows kept in the denominator: bible 99.9% (floor 90%) / canonical 99.9% (85%) / daf 98.8% / JA 74.3% (70%) / other-staged 78.7% (80%, the ONLY miss) / overall 95.0% (75%). Bible is 70% of the eligible population and is addressed by CHAPTER (verse grain was never built — the staged versemaps index the readability body, so per-verse units need the body-to-norm_stream bridge; now an explicit A0b owner question rather than an assumption). **Five findings.** (1) NEW BLOCKER-CLASS Contract 0 — the coordinate basis is UNPINNED: the asset's meta records source_db and crosswalk hashes but NO reference-corpus hash, and the locus build's own reference_corpus_sha256 is compared to nothing; agreement today is strong (0 of 214,132 public work-side offsets fall outside their work's stream, over 572 works witnessed past their midpoint) but that is evidence, not a contract — one reference refresh moves every citation silently. Fix is cheap: record the hash, carry the locus build's, assert equality, mutation-prove (~2-3 d in the CD batch). (2) The exclusion ledger is ONE cause: all 1,413 uncited eligible rows come from 22 works `build_sefaria` skipped fail-closed (zero from a missing crosswalk entry — all 613 public works have one, so the ASSOCIATION gate has nothing to repair on the public asset); the largest line is הגדה של פסח at 393 rows, skipped because `_sefaria_section_units` fails closed on non-Hebrew section labels — a fact its own docstring records — so ~15 section names clear it, and the branch fix lifts other-staged to ~99%. (3) The too-broad gate has TEETH and fails exactly two cells: other-staged/chapter 36.0% and canonical/chapter_halakhah 53.3% thin-fill (witnessed letters under a tenth of the cited units' extent), versus JA 0.2% / daf 2.0-2.1% / bible 5.8% / section 0.9%. Those two cells ARE the owner's quoter works — הלכות גדולות 182 of its 210 cited rows, ילקוט שמעוני 38 of 66 over 952 units, chapter_halakhah is משנה תורה — so the granularity gate and the quotation problem are one phenomenon seen twice, and the region naming pass is the shared remedy. (4) The audit frame's fifth stratum does NOT exist: there are no M-source works on the public asset (all 656 are identity-private and pruned). The real fact an earlier reading confused it with: 235 PUBLIC works draw their reference stream from that corpus while their identity is open, carrying 20,972 of 28,464 eligible rows. Frame corrected to bible / canonical / daf / JA / other-staged, with a stated precedence (language first, content second) because Arabic tafsir of Bible belongs to both. (5) NEGATIVE RESULT, recorded so it is not rediscovered: granularity is NOT a relation signal. Fill ratio looks strong on the graded rows (81.7% FALSE under 5% fill; median fill 27% for `correct` vs 6% for `quote_shared`) but it is length ÷ unit size, and `letters < 300` alone dominates it (81.0% precision at 88.0% recall vs 82.6% at 62.9%); in the one cell where fill knows something length does not — long AND thin, n=43 — it is 44.2% precise and holds 21 `correct` rows. **The locus addresses; it does not judge.** The matrix keeps its four inputs (router verdict, QUOTER flag, region flag, coverage band = the length axis), and the locus's only contribution to relation honesty is making REGIONS computable. **Next actionable step is unchanged in shape but now numeric: the A0a rulings + region naming pass, then A0b over these numbers.**
  - **Contract 1 was MEASURED before being built (2026-08-11) and its bar is both UNREACHABLE and UNMEASURABLE as written** — `_tmp/CONTRACT1-matrix-findings.md`; plan amended to v9 with a new phase A0c. **(a) Three of the four matrix inputs are nearly empty on the asset they will run against**: QUOTER reaches 1,004 main-pool rows (3.5%), the router 182 (0.6%), coverage-unknown 147 (0.5%), the region flag 0 (until the owner's naming pass); the whole matrix moves 1,291 of 28,464 main-pool rows (4.5%), 978 from QUOTER alone. The honest router predicate at the identification grain is 'no shipped evidence at all' = **9 rows**; the tempting 'any evidence is review_only' reading flags 4,070 and is a display-selection artifact. Coverage has NO resolution below 10% (zero rows in the 0-10% bands, 75.9% at or above 50%) because the router consumed that population upstream — it contributes known/unknown, not a band. **(b) The bar is unreachable**: across five QUOTER thresholds and eight rule variants (gating on multifolio / page count / match length, with and without the fail-closed coverage rule), scored per surface against the baseline each actually renders, NOTHING comes near the ≤2-point recall bar — findings 81.8% false-direct reduction at 58.3% true-witness loss (div≥20%), 27.3% at 11.1% (div≥50%), 0.0% at 5.6% (div≥60%, where the rule is ANTI-correlated: six true witnesses demoted, not one false one); expansion 54.5% at 35.7% (div≥50%). Monotone frontier, no knee. **Why:** all 11 false-direct rows are `main_multifolio` with 48-92% coverage and 127-1,069 matched letters — structurally indistinguishable from true witnesses on every axis the asset carries — on works whose divergence shares straddle every threshold (17%-56%: הלכות גדולות, טור, ספר המצוות, רי"ף, ספר הערוך, בבלי שבת). What is true of each is that the manuscript shares a specific PART of the work, so **only a region can reach them — the region flag is promoted from one-of-four inputs to the load-bearing one, and the owner's 1-2h naming pass is on the critical path.** **(c) The bar is also unmeasurable**: only 120 of the 1,402 blind grades (8.6%) reach a public main-pool row and 11 of those are false (41.6% of grades are on works with no crosswalk entry, 29.4% on non-public identifications, 13.2% on M-source-identity works), so by the plan's OWN sparse-cell rule (n>=30 or report-only) the entire public main-pool population is a sparse cell. The frozen 400 shares the defect — drawn from the producer population, not from what the public asset renders — so it is retained as signal-development data and is no longer the release gate. **NEW PHASE A0c**: ~200 rows sampled from what the public asset RENDERS, stratified with recorded weights and risk oversampling to carry 30+ false-direct rows, graded blind (~3-5 owner-hours) — not new effort but Contract 3's audit frame moved earlier, serving B's baseline + A0b's ratification + the audit. **(d) A priority inversion, measured:** the findings page is 9.2% FALSE on this sample while the expansion pane is 55.9% FALSE with 61 of its 102 graded rows rendering 'direct witness' — main_pool is doing real work as a filter, and the surface with no honesty machinery is the one showing what the filter rejected without saying so. **(e) The aggregate rule is grain-dependent and its direction FLIPS**: 7,587 identifications (13.7%) have displayed claims of more than one claim_type and 6,449 store direct_witness over a mix; a blanket 'most conservative member' rule would have demoted 6,449 TRUE assertions, because one identification is one manuscript x one work over several pages and a manuscript witnessing the work on ANY page IS a witness. Conservative aggregation belongs to MULTI-WORK grouped rows only.
  - **CATALOGUE YARDSTICK built (2026-08-11, owner's suggestion) — it gives a 70x larger measurable population at zero owner cost, and it is a YARDSTICK, not acceptance evidence** (the standing rule holds: adjudication stays catalogue-blind). `same_work_spike/probe/scripts/catalogue_yardstick.py` + `..._report.py`. Compares each identification's work against `libraries.csv` `titles_non_placeholder` for the same manuscript. **Coverage:** 192,426 of 255,725 catalogue rows carry a title; 86.1% of main-pool identifications have one. **Four outcomes, because three would lie:** agree_work 2,858 (10.0%) / agree_family 11,710 (41.1%, the catalogue names a container such as `משנה סדר זרעים` or `נביאים ראשונים` and our claim is more specific — agreement, not error) / disagree 761 (2.7%) / silent 9,181 (32.3%) / no title 3,954 (13.9%). **DECIDED = 15,329 main-pool rows against the 219 the graded set reaches — and agreement runs 95.0%** (bible 97.1%, canonical 85.7%, commentary 89.5%, liturgy 94.7%, other 70.2%). **Calibrated against the owner's grades** on the 219-row overlap: agree_family **0 of 34 false**, agree_work 19.0% false, disagree 50.0% false, silent 37.1%, no title 41.2% — so catalogue disagreement is a real but MODEST error detector (50% precision against a 31% base rate), while family-level agreement is a strong safe-signal. **Four limits, each measured rather than assumed.** (1) The catalogue describes the MANUSCRIPT, not the page-level relation: 4 rows where it names our work and the grade is still false are genuine `לקוטים מהלכות הרי"ף` manuscripts whose graded page is a quotation — **catalogue agreement licenses the WORK, never the relation**. (2) A tractate name is not a work name: `ראש השנה` belongs equally to בבלי, משנה and רי"ף, and the first version called a Bavli manuscript agreement with our רי"ף claim — the same-sugya confusion appearing INSIDE the yardstick. Now a corpus-conflict check demotes those to disagree, but the canonical/commentary families stay coarse exactly where the errors live. (3) **Entangled, so it cannot score QUOTER:** `novelty_status` is itself an LLM comparison against catalogue data — cross-tab shows `diverges_work` at 40.1% catalogue-disagreement against `confirms` at 3.1% — so scoring the QUOTER flag (a per-work aggregate of divergence) on this yardstick would be near-tautological, the same trap as the mask rule that scored 0/351 by construction. It is honest for signals that do NOT derive from it: coverage, length, the router, and the region flag once it exists. (4) It decides only 54% of main-pool rows. **The practical win is A0c stratification:** the 761 disagreeing rows carry ~50% false against a ~31% base, so sampling there finds errors faster and the approved 200-row pass buys more measurement per owner-hour; and agree_family (11,710 rows, 0 of 34 false) is a cheap safe-list. **Three matcher bugs were found by reading its own sanity samples, not by reasoning:** the gershayim in `תנ"ך` was replaced by a SPACE so no Bible row could match its prefix (47% of rows were called DISAGREE on that alone); a substring fallback let `תלמוד` match `התלמוד` and manufacture agreement; and generic words (`כתאב`, `התלמוד`, `התורה`) were treated as distinctive work names until a one-owner filter dropped 154 ambiguous ones. **The design fix that mattered most: DISAGREE requires the catalogue to positively name a DIFFERENT known work — silence is not disagreement**, and treating it as such is what produced an absurd 94.7% disagreement rate against a catalogue the owner describes as usually right.
  - **A0c re-stratified with the yardstick + session HANDOFF written (2026-08-11).** The approved 220-row sample now stratifies on THREE axes — surface x family x catalogue verdict (`same_work_spike/probe/scripts/a0c_sample.py`, frame `a0c-2026-08-11-v2`) — raising expected graded-FALSE rows from ~67 to **~103 of the same 220**, i.e. ~54% more measurement per owner-hour, with inverse-probability weights per stratum so every population estimate reweights back. Cell floor is 7 (24 cells), chosen because it is the largest floor that fits the approved total while still giving 21 rows per surface-x-family once the catalogue buckets aggregate — the n>=20 the frame actually requires, since its cells are FAMILIES and the catalogue axis is an overlay, not a partition to claim on. A first pass at floor 12 silently produced 288 rows: **a sample larger than the owner approved is not a free upgrade, it is unapproved owner hours**, so the allocator now asserts against the target. The catalogue verdict is a SAMPLING axis only and must not appear in the grader packet — adjudication stays catalogue-blind. Handoff with the full phase-by-phase state, the two owner-blocked items, the five unblocked engineering steps in order, and the gate commands: `_tmp/HANDOFF-granularity-2026-08-11.md`.
  - **OWNER RULINGS 2026-08-11 (plan v10) + the two instruments they unblocked.** **(1) Bible grain: SHIP CHAPTER** — verse grain stays unbuilt and uncommitted, removing the only scope question over 70% of the eligible population. **(2) NO QUOTER THRESHOLD YET** — deploy 1 ships ONLY the fail-closed corrections (9 no-shipped-evidence rows, 173 router-flagged, 147 coverage-unknown, plus the surface-grain unification) and claims no measured improvement; the region naming pass comes first because it is the one input that can reach the measured failures, and a threshold is set later over post-region numbers or not at all. **(3) A0c APPROVED at ~200 rows.** **(4) Reader-facing vocabulary: the SOFTER register** — `מתאים לחיבור` / Matches this work · `חולק טקסט` / Shares text with this work · `כולל ציטוט` / Includes a quotation · `דורש בדיקה` / Needs review. This RETIRES `Direct match`, today's constant on 28,462 of 28,464 rows, for a phrasing that does not assert identity; all four strings go through `assert_surface_honesty` and the (relation_kind -> string) pairs are pinned in `tests/test_discovery_display_strings.py`. **Instrument 1 — the region naming sheet** (`_tmp/region_naming_2026-08-11.xlsx`, 354 rows / 31 works, RTL, Hebrew instructions tab, dropdown טקסט משותף / טקסט של החיבור / לא בטוח). Built from REAL locus units, not guesses, and carrying the signal that actually fingerprints shared text: **co-citation** — the share of an individual unit's matching pages that ALSO match a different work, with those works named. Grouped so each row asks one question: (א) the 6 works carrying a graded wrong label on the findings page, unit level; (ב) 10 quoter-shaped works (thin fill ≥40% or divergence ≥60% — ילקוט ×2, ספר החינוך, תנא דבי אליהו, רש"י, תרגום יונתן); (ג) 10 liturgy works, ONE row each (whole text shared — עמידה ×5 at 92-99% co-citation, ברכות שמע, ברכת המזון); (ד) 5 Bible books whose liturgical passages are the classic shared text. A per-work `— כל החיבור —` row allows a whole-work ruling; blanks fall to the safe side. **Instrument 2 — the A0c sample, FROZEN** (`_tmp/a0c_sample_2026-08-11.json`, frame_version `a0c-2026-08-11`): 220 rows, 10 strata (2 surfaces × 5 families), n≥20 per cell (findings|bible 40), deterministic seeded-sha256 draw, inverse-probability weights recorded per stratum (44.6 to 714.0), the three risk overlays tagged (multifolio 100 / divergent 54 / coverage_unknown 35), and each row's baseline rendered label captured AT DRAW TIME so the comparison cannot drift. The expansion surface is deliberately oversampled — it carries ~56% false vs the findings page's ~9% — giving ~67 expected false-direct rows against the n≥30 floor; **an oversample without recorded weights would be a biased release claim, so every population estimate must be reweighted.** Remaining before grading: render the deck (existing `scripts/cert01_render_deck.py` machinery).
  - **The region naming instrument is now an HTML reviewer that SHOWS THE TEXT (2026-08-11), replacing the spreadsheet** — owner feedback: a region ruling is a judgement about text and a spreadsheet cell cannot show text with the matched passages marked inside it. Builder + smoke test live with the other deck builders (`same_work_spike/probe/scripts/build_region_deck.py`, `region_deck_html.py`, `region_deck_smoke.js`); output is `_tmp/region_reviewer.html` — 31 works, 276 unit cards + 31 whole-work rows = 307 rulings, 1.1 MB. **NEVER UPLOADED OR PUBLISHED: it embeds restricted reference-corpus text and is written for local viewing only** (the standing audit-deck rule). **CORRECTED 2026-08-11 — the first version showed the space-free normalized stream and claimed the spaced editions were unreachable; that was WRONG, and the owner said so by pointing at the existing reviewers.** They solve it: `build_smoke_preview2.RefText` reads the RAW source files and maps stream offsets back through `norm_stream`'s offset array. Measured while rebuilding: all 1,390 locus works resolve to a raw file (1,227 directly, 163 through a monolith parent, none unresolved) and the corpus stream reproduces the raw file EXACTLY (60/60 on the spot check) — so the mapping is exact and needs no fuzzy alignment, unlike `RefText.passage`, which aligns only because it starts from a page slice rather than a known unit offset. Manuscript text likewise: 3,000/3,000 sampled claim pages are present in the full-corpus `pages` table with spaced HTR text (the smoke DB has a different page population — 0/4,000 — which is what made the first attempt look impossible). **The marking measures RECURRENCE, not union, and that correction was measured:** highlighting every match covered 80-100% of the text on 63% of cards and said nothing, so a position is now marked only when enough of the unit's own identifications cover it (dark ≥50%, light ≥20%, nothing under 3 identifications — stated on the card so an unmarked unit is never read as a cold one). After the change: 28.3% of cards at 80-100%, 20.7% at 10-40%, 23.9% honestly unmarked. A clipped unit's recurrence count says `(בחלון המוצג)` rather than letting a window figure read as a whole-unit fact. **Verification, since the Chrome extension was not connected and the deck could NOT be render-tested:** the deck's real script is executed under node against a DOM stub, asserting works/unit-rows/button counts, unique `data-uid`s, both heat levels present, and a ruling round-tripping through localStorage AND the download payload including toggle-off; the gate is **proven able to fail** — three mutants (renamed `data-v`, gutted store write, declassed `<em>`) each fail it, and the heat change itself broke the old assertion, which is how it was caught. Quoting hazards were removed by construction rather than tested for: no interpolated ids in inline handlers (ONE delegated listener), a uid→element Map instead of a CSS attribute selector, all HTML escaping done in Python. Rulings download as JSON (`region_rulings.json`) and reload — `file://` localStorage is not a safe store. The xlsx (`_tmp/region_naming_2026-08-11.xlsx`) remains as a record but is superseded for the pass itself.
  - **Region reviewer v2 — the deck model, both sides readable, one manuscript at a time (2026-08-11).** Rebuilt on the existing reviewers' shape per owner direction: **223 cards, one at a time**, each card ONE unit of one work with two panes — the unit's own edition text (spaced, punctuated, from the raw file via the offset map) and **one source manuscript at a time** with its matched passage marked, the other manuscripts reachable by arrows (1,130 manuscript panes across the deck; shelfmark, folio and matched-letter count on each; the graded-wrong case badged where there is one). Rulings are per unit, keyboard-driven (1/2/3, arrows for cards, n/p for manuscripts), autosaved and downloadable. **Two real defects found and fixed during the rebuild, both by measurement rather than inspection:** (1) marking the UNION of matches covered 80-100% of the text on 63% of cards and said nothing, so marking now measures RECURRENCE — dark ≥50% of the unit's own identifications, light ≥20%, nothing under 3, stated on the card; (2) the clip window centred on the MEAN of match midpoints, which cut the hot passage out whenever matches scattered — group-א cards reported a peak at 60% of their identifications while showing no marking at all. The window now centres on the densest position; the count of cards hiding their own peak went from many to **0**. Behaviour now validates against expectation: prayer units light up (46 of 53 cards carry strong recurrence, median hot passage 269 chars) while Bible chapters almost never do (1 of 30) — manuscripts witness a Bible chapter at different verses rather than converging on one passage, which is the right answer and tells the owner where a region does NOT exist. **Verification: still no browser** (the extension is not connected), so the deck's real script runs under node against a DOM stub — asserting both panes render, the reference text is SPACED, a passage is marked on the manuscript side, both heat levels exist, the manuscript pager and card navigation move, and a ruling round-trips through localStorage and the download including toggle-off. **The gate is proven able to fail: 5/5 mutants caught** (frozen pager, dropped store write, frozen card nav, removed `<mark>`, declassed `<em>`) — and the fifth was ADDED after a mutant escaped, because a regex over `h[12]` still passes when one level is lost. Also caught in passing: an inline bash patch mangled a template literal into `includes()` with no argument, making one assertion vacuous — found by reading the patched file, fixed with a proper edit.
  - **Region reviewer v3 — THE TWO PANES WERE SHOWING DIFFERENT PASSAGES; fixed, and now gated by a measurement (2026-08-11).** Owner caught it from a screenshot: the edition pane read a Ketubot/mourning sugya while the manuscript pane read blessings. **Cause:** the edition window was chosen ONCE PER UNIT (centred on the unit's densest recurrence) while the manuscript pane is per manuscript — and units are enormous, הלכות גדולות סימן א alone being **109,486 characters**, so the two panes routinely landed on unrelated sugyas of the same siman. It was invisible to every gate in place, because each pane was individually correct. **Fix:** the work-side offsets of the SAME producer alignment (`w_start`/`w_end`, the coherent four-column pair with `aligned_page_start`/`aligned_page_end`) are now carried per manuscript, and the edition window follows the selected manuscript — flipping manuscripts moves both panes together. The manuscript's own matched span is underlined inside the edition text (`mine`), distinct from the recurrence background, so a reader can see both what THIS witness matched and how often OTHERS matched the same place. **The new gate is quantitative, not visual** (`same_work_spike/probe/scripts/region_deck_parallel_check.py`): it extracts the marked text from each side, normalizes both to the letter stream, and scores similarity — **median 84.6, 99.6% of 1,130 panes at or above 60, none below 40**. The four panes under 60 are unit-boundary clips (a 17-letter edition span against a 232-letter manuscript span), which is honest rather than misaligned. **Proven able to fail:** the mutant that rotates edition windows by one within each card — the owner's defect, reproduced — drops it to 59.9% at-or-above-60 and trips two of the three conditions (`region_deck_parallel_mutant.py`). Note the median only fell to 76.0, so a median-only gate would have MISSED it; the share floor and the sub-40 check are what catch it. The node smoke gate grew two paired-pane assertions and now catches **6/6** mutants (`region_deck_mutants.py`). **Two harness defects found along the way, both mine:** an inline-bash patch to the parallel checker silently failed to apply, so it kept reading the original deck and reported the rotation mutant as MISSED — the gate was fine, the harness was inert; and a literal `class='h2'` mutation left the combined `class='h2 mine'` chunks intact, so the mutation had to be made faithful before it could test anything. Both are the same lesson twice: **a mutation that does not change the artifact proves nothing about the gate.**
  - **Region reviewer v4 — the CATALOGUE TITLES are now in it, at both grains (2026-08-11, owner: "if I can use why not you").** The catalogue-blind rule is about ADJUDICATION, and the region pass is not adjudication — it is curation, and what the matching manuscripts ARE is the direct evidence for whether a unit is shared text. So: **region pass = catalogue VISIBLE; A0c relation grading = catalogue BLIND**, and that line is now written into `catalogue_match.py`'s docstring rather than left to memory. The matcher was extracted from `catalogue_yardstick.py` into side-effect-free `catalogue_match.py` so the reviewer can use it without running the yardstick's queries at import (identical numbers after the split). **Two grains, because one would not decide the question:** each manuscript pane shows its own catalogue title plus a colour-coded verdict against the work (same work / same corpus / different work / not identified), and each CARD shows a summary over EVERY manuscript that matched the unit (up to 60, not only the six displayed) with the titles named and the share the catalogue calls a different work. **That per-unit share is the region signal in a number, and it discriminates sharply:** 143 of 223 cards get a verdict; 78 sit at 0% and 42 at 75-100%; by group the medians are prayer 100%, wrong-label 17%, quoting works 14%, Bible 0% (a Bible fragment is catalogued as Bible, so the signal correctly says those units are the work's own text). The extreme cases now decide themselves — ילקוט שמעוני רמז תתקלח draws manuscripts catalogued פיוט ×9 / הגדה של פסח ×6 / תוספות של סידור ×3, which is the owner's own זמנים-haggadah model appearing in one line; טור אורח חיים סימן תקעט draws פיוט ×7 / תפילה וברכות ×6. **Also clarified in the deck's own text, since the owner read it as ambiguous: the ruling is on the WORK'S UNIT, never on a manuscript — the manuscripts are the evidence.** Gates: smoke grew four catalogue assertions and now catches **8/8** mutants (two new ones drop the manuscript title and the per-unit summary). **Third inert-harness bug found and fixed:** `region_deck_mutants.py` resolved its output directory from an unexported `CLAUDE_JOB_DIR` to a `.	mp` that does not exist, so the whole harness crashed rather than testing anything — and two attempts to patch it inline in bash silently failed to match before an editor was used. Same lesson a third time: do not inline tricky content in bash, and read the patched file back.

The byte-exactness needed one thing — **rows sorted by `LineNumber` within each page**; in array order three documents come out transposed, identical in length to the character, which is why a length check saw nothing and they failed closed. Two defects found while building it, both invisible to a unit count: (1) an empty page and the page after it share a start offset, and the generic deduper keeps the FIRST, so **2,302 real pages across 46 of 92 works** would have published their text under the preceding empty page's number — an off-by-a-page that passes every invariant; the emptiness test is now explicit and precedes the dedupe. (2) One real heading, `319א. כז א- יט [28- 27]`, carries a **bracketed decimal pair**, the one shape the surface's interval scanner rejects for the WHOLE envelope — the previous defence was a comment asserting a list of Hebrew sigla is never a decimal pair, which is true of the sigla and false of the corpus. The builder now unwraps that shape (598 sigla brackets untouched) and `check_invariants` fails the build on any survivor. Verified over the family: 2,144 rendered citations, 0 running backwards, 0 bracketed pairs; page numbers ascend in all 89, and a work whose numbering did not would fall back to the marker grain rather than ship a reversed range.
- ✅ **Resolved (2026-08-10) — the full partition tree is harvested and bound, so Judeo-Arabic section names and their HIERARCHY now come from the publisher rather than from inference.** 92/92 works, **26,031 nodes**, 0 failures, read from the tree control's own node objects after expanding every load-on-demand node (depths `{0: 2497, 1: 18465, 2: 5069}`; no work came back short of the id-gap prediction). Bound to positions by aligning the node sequence against the marker sequence — both are in reading order, the tree provably so — giving **24,791 of 24,877 markers (99.7%)** a publisher node and leaving only 184 of 26,031 nodes unplaceable. **7,755 page addresses in 37 of 89 works now name a parent the publisher STATES** (52.1%, up from 8.0% on the top-level-only harvest); chain depths went 2,431/542 shallow to **17,294 at depth 2 and 5,058 at depth 3**. Result reads as scholarship: `מסכת בבא קמא, פרק ראשון, עמ' 8`, `עיטורי השירה, שער ההשאלה, עמ' 226`. Four defects found and fixed in the process: (1) **the source filenames are NOT the site's title ids throughout** — two documents were handed each other's trees (`אגרות שמואל בן עלי` given `ספר המאזניים`'s section names and vice versa, each aligning 0.000 with what it was given and 1.000 with the other's); the filename binding is now a claim that is checked by alignment and repaired by search, ranked on labels MATCHED not the fraction of the tree matched (one 1-node tree scores a perfect fraction against three unrelated works) and requiring a 2x margin over the runner-up, because two volumes of one commentary have near-identical structures and a coin-toss there would publish one volume's section names over the other's text with every offset still correct. (2) The publisher's own tree text embeds **marker syntax** — one node reads `[ספר השנים ...] +3~ [החלק הראשון]` — which reached 20 citations; stripped, along with any stray `~`. (3) The tree nests a book under its own name, so a chain read `בראשית, בראשית, א`; a link repeating its immediate parent is now dropped (a non-adjacent repeat is kept — a `הקדמה` inside discourse 1 is not the book's opening `הקדמה`). (4) Label whitespace collapses, the publisher's `פרק  א.` having become a double space mid-citation. Also recovered: the work previously written off as "organised by a principle the markers do not express" is indexed by manuscript shelfmark, and now cites as `כת"י פירקוביץ אוסף ב' 72, אגרת [א], עמ' 31`. Two works still refuse the alignment under the filename binding alone and are the two that got rebound; **0 works end up unbound**. Remaining: label length has a long tail (median 25, p95 49, **max 144**) from genuinely long publisher headings — the artifact keeps them whole and any ellipsis is a surface decision, not a builder one; and one heading legitimately contains `מעמ' 232`, so a section name can mention a page other than the one being cited.
- ✅ **Fixed (2026-08-10, commits `dba41861` + `f1d07d18`) — the SECOND scholar-audit pass found four more locus defects, and three of them were one bug: the header parser insisted a numbered level be called `פרק`.** Owner judged 64 of 353 cards (43 correct / 20 wrong / 1 skip). Of the 20 wrong, **6 were locus defects and are fixed; 13 are the shared-quotation class (below), which is an identification defect; 1 remains open** (the Judeo-Arabic supplement heading, below).
  - **A work numbering its top level by any other word stated a perfect address and got NOTHING.** `_ADDR_RE` hardcoded `פרק`, so `סדר התפילה, הלכה א`, `שער ב, סעיף ג` and `פרק ג, דיבור א` parsed as no address; the header emitted no unit and its text fell silently into the last `פרק` before it. Measured over the whole M-source directory: **314 headers in 8 works, 154,373 letters.** The owner met it three times on one deck as `הלכות מילה, פרק ג` printed over the whole of `סדר התפילה` — **20.3% of משנה תורה ספר אהבה** — and marked it *"crucial!"*.
  - **THE UNADDRESSED WORKS WERE THE CLASSICAL MIDRASHIM.** תנחומא, בראשית רבה, ויקרא רבה, פסיקתא דרב כהנא, מכילתא, ספרי דברים, איכה רבה, שאילתות, ארבעה טורים, שערי תשובה. **824 → 1,390 works, 55,457 → 71,159 units, none lost.** Crosswalk-reachable work ids 754 → 952; **7,087 more evidence rows can be addressed (91.9% → 94.7%)**. Every label is copied off the header verbatim: `בראשית, פסקה א`, `פרשה א, פסקה א`, and פסיקתא by the piska's own incipit (`ויהי ביום כלות, פסקה א`).
  - **The counting word is now always said, which was the audit's other request** — *"we should call it פרק ד"*, *"should say פרק ב"*, *"פרק לד"*. 156,978 addressable rows read differently. `shorten_range_tail` already elided the repeat, so a range still reads `פרק קכט–קל`.
  - **The gate then found a defect on 13 works that NOTHING in this change created** — it was unreachable until the parser grew wide enough to build them. `division_ord` mapped the EMPTY division onto slot 0, which is also the first NAMED division's slot: harmless while a work had divisions everywhere or nowhere, wrong the moment one has both, which is the ordinary shape of a שער opening with text before its first פרק. A slot is now one numbering SYSTEM — the division AND the word it counts by — because two counting words are two numberings just as surely as two books are, and `M:Ytext11000` states both `פרק א` and `פסוק א`.
  - **Judeo-Arabic: a section opening MID-PAGE was published under the page's first name.** The chain was sampled at the page START, so every letter after a mid-page marker carried the previous section's name — and nothing about it looks wrong from outside: the page number is right, the offsets are right, the reader is told the wrong פצל. **2,858 of 14,885 pages hold such a change; 1,482,431 letters sat after one.** A page now contributes one unit per section it holds text of. On the cards: `אלבאב אלתאסע, פצל ג, עמ' 393` → `פצל ד`, and `89. [ארו, ארט, ג], עמ' 56` → `91. [פ, לו]`, the marker the owner quoted. A third card nobody flagged moves too. 14,885 → 19,620 JA units; distinct (work, section) pairs 3,829 → 5,964, so nothing collapsed.
  - **The ascending-check relaxation is load-bearing, proven by mutation on the real corpus:** with the strict check restored, only **10 of 89** works still build by printed page and **79 fall back** to the weaker marker grain — 6,915 units instead of 19,620, a total regression wearing a fail-closed's clothes. A tie is admitted only where the section changes; the same page twice under one section is still refused.
  - Gates: 308 locus tests, ruff clean, masking `--scan-repo` exit 0, invariants clean. Deck regenerated ONCE over the corrected artifact (378 cards; 60 of 64 judgements force-carried, **40 held / 20 restated / 4 orphaned**; deck verifier reports 0 address/piece misalignments).
  - **9 of 17 — the work has ZERO mask intervals, and 9 of those 9 are `cat=Sefaria`.** Exactly what the 2026-08-09 staleness entry above predicts: `ref_canon_masks_v2.json` was written 2026-07-10, `ref2_build.py` expanded the corpus on 2026-07-15 with 247 undocumented `REF2:sef_*` works, and the masker was never re-run. Cards: תנא דבי אליהו רבה + זוטא, ילקוט שמעוני, ספר הערוך ×2, רי"ף, רבנו חננאל ×3. **That entry rested on one owner-reported case (Isaiah 35:10 in the Tur); this is nine more, drawn blind by a stratified sample.**

This is distinguishable from the documented hull-extension hole, where the span starts outside a mask and runs THROUGH it: here the span does not touch a mask interval at all, so it is canonical-sweep **recall**, not seed-vs-hull. 4 of the 6 are `cat=JA`.
  - **2 of 17 — outside the mechanism by construction, not by staleness.** `mask_ref_canon.py:179-180` reads `canon = [w for w in works if w['cat'] in CANON_CATS]` / `edited = [w for w in works if w['cat'] not in CANON_CATS]`, so **a canonical work is never a masking TARGET**: the Bavli quoting a verse (card 27) and the Yerushalmi quoting the Mishnah (card 23) cannot be masked. The deck-level canonical-rendering guard reaches the same outcome deliberately, via its **claimed-work exemption** (a match survives if the claimed work is itself canonical). So these two cards are a complaint against a deliberate exemption, not a bug — and card 23 is additionally already ruled: the Q2 adjudication ontology's family 4 (sibling codes / parallel sugyot, naming משנה↔ירושלמי) assigns verdict **B**, identified by structure rather than by the shared passage. **Not to be re-litigated here; surfaced because the owner has now objected to the exemption twice.**
  - **The one thing that is genuinely new: the source MARKS its own quotations, and nothing has ever read the marks.** M-source writes `"…"` around a quoted verse or mishnah and `{…}` around an editor's lemma. `norm_stream` drops them at the same step and by the same rule as nikud or a comma, and its offset array records only LETTER positions — so the delimiter position is not recoverable from the stream. Confirmed across both corpora's matching path: `{`, `}`, `!`, `⟦`, `⟧`, `>>` appear as a named case nowhere in the research tree, and where `#…#` WAS noticed (`ref_header_bug.md`) the ruling was that it is a **content** convention whose wrapped letters must be kept byte-identical. Every existing mechanism infers quotation status statistically — an n-gram sweep against a canon index (a 16-hour full pass), page coverage, IDF rarity, or introducer formulae. **None of them reads what the editor wrote down.**
  - **And the signal separates the owner's own judgements.** Over the judged cards on standalone M-source works (n=15): the 4 he called shared quotation have a **median 0.94** of their span inside quotation markup; the 9 he called correct have **median 0.00, max 0.32, none over 0.5**. Corpus-wide, **9,299 of 39,648 addressable M-source spans (23.5%) sit more than half inside markup**, bimodally — 21,089 under 0.1 against 5,590 over 0.8. Consistent with, and smaller than, `mapv2_tierA_verse_audit.md`'s 42,711/110,068 (38.8%) canon-verse rows misattributed to a non-canonical work (different denominator and method). **Its attraction is that it cannot go stale** — it is in the text, so it needs no re-run when the corpus grows, which is the precise failure mode of the adopted mechanism.
  - **Its limit, stated:** one family marks only the LEMMA word. `רבנו חננאל על מסכת סוכה` writes `!מוכרי! ספרים ותפילין…` and the quoted mishnah continues unmarked, so that card scores 0.00 and no markup rule reaches it. A markup signal would separate most of the class, never all of it.
  - **Not a locus defect and not fixable in the locus stage.** The locus faithfully reports where the matched text sits; whether the match should exist belongs to identification. Do NOT re-propose edge-classification or DF-cap-only (both tried and rejected, `METHOD.md` §9.2), and do NOT mask testimony — the standing ruling is that testimony and citation are "two products, opposite handling", and only low-coverage citation is discovery noise. Related open items: the stale mask file and the unwired `CanonRarity` score, both 2026-08-09 above.

Keying on the work family and checking "fits the largest Bible book" lets בראשית רבתי through at **93**, the one case that must not pass. Both were probed before either was believed.

A second trap the tests pinned: three families carry no corpus prefix (`targum_onkelos_genesis`, not `sef_…`), and `sefaria_unit_word`'s `b2_` fallback rewrites only a leading `sef_` — a guessed prefix matches nothing and fails **silently**, the work merely looking unlisted.

(2) Worse: **11 of 26 showed more pieces than the address named, or in the wrong order** (`address ב; ד` against panes `['ד','ב']`; one card with 13 panes for a 5-run citation), because the deck built a pane per raw alignment and predated `merge_witnessed_spans` — a reader comparing address to panes would conclude the address had DROPPED pieces, pushing the judgement the opposite way from defect (1). Cost of the fix: 4 of 39 recorded verdicts orphaned, all scattered cards (3 skips carrying notes and 1 `correct` judged against a possibly-mismatched pane set), because refusing mismatched cards changed the sample.
